#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block cooperatively loads coalesced tiles, then each thread accumulates
+// several output elements in registers. All accumulation remains in double
+// precision and visits k in the same order as the original multiplication.
+template<int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, int N, int rows) {
+    constexpr int KTile = 16;
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }

+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);

+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }

+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;

+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;

+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }

+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }

+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);

+        }

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char* value = argv[++i];
            char* end = nullptr;
            errno = 0;
            const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(value, &end, 10);
            if (value[0] == '-' || end == value || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE ||
                parsed == 0 || parsed > INT_MAX ||
                parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / parsed / sizeof(double)) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size must be a positive integer that fits in memory.\n");
                return 1;
            }
            N = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    try {
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);

    // Allocation, input transfers and CUDA module loading are preparation;
    // time the computation after every device has its input matrices.
    CudaMatrixMultiply multiplication(A, B, static_cast<int>(N));
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    multiplication.multiply();
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", seconds * 1000.0);
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / seconds / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);

    multiplication.copyResult(C);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }

    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Matrix multiplication failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block cooperatively loads coalesced tiles, then each thread accumulates
+// several output elements in registers. All accumulation remains in double
+// precision and visits k in the same order as the original multiplication.
+template<int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, int N, int rows) {
+    constexpr int KTile = 16;
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block cooperatively loads coalesced tiles, then each thread accumulates
+// several output elements in registers. All accumulation remains in double
+// precision and visits k in the same order as the original multiplication.
+template<int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, int N, int rows) {
+    constexpr int KTile = 16;
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }

+    template<int Tile>

+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;

+        } else {

+        }

+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);

+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block cooperatively loads coalesced tiles, then each thread accumulates
+// several output elements in registers. All accumulation remains in double
+// precision and visits k in the same order as the original multiplication.
+template<int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, int N, int rows) {
+    constexpr int KTile = 16;
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

+                #pragma unroll

+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];

-            C[i * N + j] = sum;

+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+namespace {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block cooperatively loads coalesced tiles, then each thread accumulates
+// several output elements in registers. All accumulation remains in double
+// precision and visits k in the same order as the original multiplication.
+template<int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, int N, int rows) {
+    constexpr int KTile = 16;
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block cooperatively loads coalesced tiles, then each thread accumulates
+// several output elements in registers. All accumulation remains in double
+// precision and visits k in the same order as the original multiplication.
+template<int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void multiplyTiles(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                              const double* __restrict__ B,
+                              double* __restrict__ C, int N, int rows) {
+    constexpr int KTile = 16;
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][KTile + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[KTile][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int tid = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const int rowBase = blockIdx.y * Tile;
+    const int colBase = blockIdx.x * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (int base = 0; base < N; base += KTile) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int offset = tid; offset < Tile * KTile; offset += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int ar = offset / KTile;
+            const int ak = offset % KTile;
+            const int bk = offset / Tile;
+            const int bc = offset % Tile;
+            tileA[ar][ak] = (FullTiles || (rowBase + ar < rows && base + ak < N))
+                ? A[static_cast<size_t>(rowBase + ar) * N + base + ak] : 0.0;
+            tileB[bk][bc] = (FullTiles || (base + bk < N && colBase + bc < N))
+                ? B[static_cast<size_t>(base + bk) * N + colBase + bc] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < KTile; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                a[r] = tileA[ty + r * Threads][k];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                b[c] = tileB[k][tx + c * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+                    sums[r][c] += a[r] * b[c];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < Results; ++r) {
+        const int row = rowBase + ty + r * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int c = 0; c < Results; ++c) {
+            const int col = colBase + tx + c * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[static_cast<size_t>(row) * N + col] = sums[r][c];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+struct DevicePartition {
+    int device;
+    int firstRow;
+    int rows;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* a = nullptr;
+    double* b = nullptr;
+    double* c = nullptr;
+
+    DevicePartition(int device, int firstRow, int rows)
+        : device(device), firstRow(firstRow), rows(rows) {}
+
+    DevicePartition(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+    DevicePartition& operator=(const DevicePartition&) = delete;
+
+    ~DevicePartition() {
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (a) cudaFree(a);
+        if (b) cudaFree(b);
+        if (c) cudaFree(c);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+
+    void prepare(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "Create CUDA stream");
+        const size_t rowBytes = static_cast<size_t>(rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+        const size_t matrixBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N * sizeof(double);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&a, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&b, matrixBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix B");
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&c, rowBytes), "Allocate CUDA matrix C");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(a, A.data() + static_cast<size_t>(firstRow) * N,
+                                  rowBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix A");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(b, B.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream), "Upload matrix B");
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void loadKernel(int N) {
+        // Load the selected kernel before starting the computation timer.
+        cudaFuncAttributes attributes;
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, true>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        } else {
+            checkCuda(cudaFuncGetAttributes(&attributes, multiplyTiles<Tile, false>), "Load CUDA kernel");
+        }
+    }
+
+    template<int Tile>
+    void launch(int N) {
+        const dim3 block(16, 16);
+        const dim3 grid((N + Tile - 1) / Tile, (rows + Tile - 1) / Tile);
+        if (N % Tile == 0 && rows % Tile == 0) {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        } else {
+            multiplyTiles<Tile, false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(a, b, c, N, rows);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launch CUDA matrix multiplication");
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Select CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "Complete CUDA operation");
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatrixMultiply {
+    int N;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DevicePartition>> partitions;
+
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, int N) : N(N) {
+        int deviceCount = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "Query CUDA devices");
+        if (deviceCount == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("CUDA matrix multiplication requires a CUDA-capable GPU");
+        }
+
+        // Keep at least 1024 rows per GPU to amortize multi-device overhead.
+        // Row partitions require no communication during multiplication.
+        const int devices = std::min(deviceCount, std::max(1, N / 1024));
+        constexpr int RowTile = 64;
+        const int tiles = (N + RowTile - 1) / RowTile;
+        int firstTile = 0;
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            const int count = tiles / devices + (device < tiles % devices ? 1 : 0);
+            const int firstRow = firstTile * RowTile;
+            const int rows = std::min(count * RowTile, N - firstRow);
+            auto partition = std::make_unique<DevicePartition>(device, firstRow, rows);
+            partition->prepare(A, B, N);
+            if (N <= 512) partition->loadKernel<32>(N);
+            else partition->loadKernel<64>(N);
+            partitions.push_back(std::move(partition));
+            firstTile += count;
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void multiply() {
+        // Enqueue every GPU before waiting, so their kernels execute concurrently.
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            if (N <= 512) partition->launch<32>(N);
+            else partition->launch<64>(N);
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(partition->device), "Select CUDA device");
+            const size_t offset = static_cast<size_t>(partition->firstRow) * N;
+            const size_t bytes = static_cast<size_t>(partition->rows) * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + offset, partition->c, bytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, partition->stream), "Download matrix C");
+        }
+        for (auto& partition : partitions) partition->synchronize();
+    }
+};
+
+} // namespace
+
